                                    //Form1.InputBox74.Text = ""
                                    Call 发送微信消息(微信窗口句柄id, get车号() & " 号加入房间" & 房间ID & "失败，寒冰结束，请重新创建房间发送")
                                    Exit For
                                End If
                            Next
                            If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                                Delay 5000
                            End If

                            //关闭助战
                            Call 关闭助战()

                            is合作次数 = 0
                            Exit For
                        End If
                        Delay 500
                    Next

                    Exit For
                Next
            End If


            If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                好友房间号 = -1
                For 40
                    Delay 1000
                    If 判断当前是否在战场中() = 1 Then
                        Call 发送微信消息(微信窗口句柄id, get车号() & " 号成功进入[寒冰]房间号 " & 房间ID)
                        Delay 2000
                        当前在战斗 = 1
                        TracePrint "进入战场了"
                        Delay 50
                        //变量初始化
                        Call paramInit()
                        If getCard("幻精灵") = 0 Then
                            装备切换 = - 1
                        ElseIf 默认初始装备 = "无" Then
                            装备切换 = 1
                        End If
                        //合作标志 = 1
                        Call 合作标志Handle(1)
                        扩建控制 = 合作前期扩建允许次数
                        好友房间号 = 1
                        合作计时器 = Now
                        TracePrint "已进入好友房间，开始合作"

                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next
            End If


        End If

        Delay 200
        If 对战结束确定() = 1 Then
            当前在战斗 = 0
            TracePrint "好友合作子程序判断了对战结束确定"
            Call MoveToFcn(515,535)
            Delay 100
            dm.LeftClick

        End If

        If 好友房间号 <> -1 Then
            Exit Do
        End If

    Loop
End Sub

Function 鼠标向左滑动()
    Delay 1500
    Call MoveToFcn(498, 309)
    Delay 100
    dm.LeftDown
    Delay 100
    Call MoveToFcn(498-150, 309)
    Delay 100
    dm.LeftUp
End Function


Function 双开自动完成6次寒冰b()

    Dim 好友房间号, 寒冰界面1, 寒冰界面2, 活动, res, 与好友一起, 加入房间,出现房间号,加入,寒冰未打完助战,isExtFor
    Do

        Do
            好友房间号 = Form1.InputBox74.Text
            If len(好友房间号) > 0 Then
                好友房间号 = Int(好友房间号)
                If 好友房间号 > 999 Then
                    Exit Do
                Else
                    TracePrint "房间号不对，清空"
                    Form1.InputBox74.Text = ""
                End If
            End If
            Delay 2000
            TracePrint "等待房间号中"
        Loop


        If 进入寒冰主界面() = 1 Then

            For 10
                //加入房间......
                Delay 1000

                //单击冰堡挑战
                Call MoveToFcn(840,567)
                Delay 50
                dm.LeftClick
                Delay 2000


                TracePrint "识别寒冰未打完助战"
                寒冰未打完助战 = remoteFindPic("寒冰未打完助战.bmp", "353,443,433,486", 0.75, 1)
                If len(寒冰未打完助战) > 0 Then
                    TracePrint "识别寒冰未打完助战成功"
                    res = Split(寒冰未打完助战, "|")
					Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                    Delay 50
                    dm.LeftClick
                    Delay 1500
                End If


                与好友一起 = remoteFindPic("与好友一起.bmp", "286,462,395,515", 0.75, 1)
                If len(与好友一起) > 0 Then
                    res = Split(与好友一起, "|")
					Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                    Delay 50
                    dm.LeftClick
                    Delay 1500
                End If

                加入房间 = remoteFindPic("加入房间.bmp", "488,371,728,475", 0.73, 1)
                If len(加入房间) > 0 Then
                    res = Split(加入房间, "|")
					Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                    Delay 50
                    dm.LeftClick
                    Delay 1500
                    出现房间号 = 1
                End If

                For 5
                    加入 = remoteFindPic("加入.bmp|合作寒冰助战.bmp", "491,416,547,452", 0.75, 1)
                    If len(加入) > 0  Then
                        //点击输入框
                        Call MoveToFcn(504,358)
                        Delay 100
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 500
                        Call 喊话输入(好友房间号)

                        //确定助战
                        Delay 1000
                        res = Split(加入, "|")
						Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                        Delay 50
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 100

                        For 8
                            Delay 300
                            If 加入房间失败() = 1 Then
                                好友房间号 = -1
                                Form1.InputBox74.Text = ""
                                isExtFor = 1
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next
                        If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                            Delay 5000
                        End If

                        //关闭助战
                        Call 关闭助战()

                        is合作次数 = 0
                        isExtFor = 1
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next

                If isExtFor = 1 Then
                    Exit For
                End If
                Delay 500
            Next
        End If


        If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
            好友房间号 = -1
            For 40
                Delay 1000
                If 判断当前是否在战场中() = 1 Then
                    当前在战斗 = 1
                    TracePrint "进入战场了"
                    Delay 50
                    //变量初始化
                    Call paramInit()
                    If getCard("幻精灵") = 0 Then
                        装备切换 = - 1
                    ElseIf 默认初始装备 = "无" Then
                        装备切换 = 1
                    End If
                    //合作标志 = 1
                    Call 合作标志Handle(1)
                    扩建控制 = 合作前期扩建允许次数
                    好友房间号 = 1
                    TracePrint "已进入好友房间，开始合作"
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next
        End If



        Delay 200
        If 对战结束确定() = 1 Then
            当前在战斗 = 0
            TracePrint "好友合作子程序判断了对战结束确定"
            Call MoveToFcn(515,535)
            Delay 100
            dm.LeftClick

        End If

        If 好友房间号 <> -1 Then
            Exit Do
        End If


    Loop

End Function
Function 双开自动完成6次寒冰a()
    Dim 好友房间号, 寒冰界面1, 寒冰界面2,  与好友一起, 创建房间,出现房间号
    出现房间号 = 0
    Do
        If 进入寒冰主界面() = 1 Then

            For 100
                //创建房间
                Delay 1000

                //单击冰堡挑战
                Call MoveToFcn(840,567)
                Delay 50
                dm.LeftClick
                Delay 1500

                与好友一起 = remoteFindPic("与好友一起.bmp", "286,462,395,515", 0.83, 1)
                If len(与好友一起) > 0 Then
                    res = Split(与好友一起, "|")
					Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                    Delay 50
                    dm.LeftClick
                    Delay 1500
                End If

                创建房间 = remoteFindPic("创建房间.bmp", "316,368,540,519", 0.80, 1)
                If len(创建房间) > 0 Then
                   res = Split(创建房间, "|")
					Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                    Delay 50
                    dm.LeftClick
                    Delay 1500
                    出现房间号 = 1
                End If

                Exit For
            Next
        End If



        If 出现房间号 > 0 Then

            For 5
                Delay 200
                好友房间号 = remoteOcr("484, 344, 560, 374", 0,0)
                TracePrint "识别到房间号为：" & 好友房间号
                If len(好友房间号) <= 0 Then
                    //执行创建房间
                    出现房间号 = 0
                Else
                    If Int(好友房间号) < 1000 Then
                    	好友房间号 = 0 & 好友房间号
                    End If

                    Form1.InputBox74.Text = 好友房间号
                    出现房间号 = 1
                    Delay 2000
                    dm_ret = 社交频道入口(0)
                    If len (dm_ret) > 1 Then
                		res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
						Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                        Delay 50
                        dm.LeftClick
                    End If
                End If
            Next

            If 出现房间号 > 0 Then
                出现房间号 = -1
                For 180
                    Delay 1000
                    TracePrint "等待进入战场中..."
                    If 判断当前是否在战场中() = 1 Then
                        当前在战斗 = 1
                        TracePrint "进入战场了"
                        Delay 50
                        //变量初始化
                        Call paramInit()
                        If getCard("幻精灵") = 0 Then
                            装备切换 = - 1
                        ElseIf 默认初始装备 = "无" Then
                            装备切换 = 1
                        End If
                        //合作标志 = 1
                        Call 合作标志Handle(1)
                        扩建控制 = 合作前期扩建允许次数
                        TracePrint "已进入好友房间，开始合作"
                        出现房间号 = 1
                        Form1.InputBox74.Text = ""
                        Exit For
                    End If

                    If len(Form1.InputBox74.Text) < 2 Then
                        TracePrint "检查到房间号无效，重新创建"
                        出现房间号 = - 1
                        Delay 2000
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next
            End If
        End If

        Delay 200
        If 对战结束确定() = 1 Then
            当前在战斗 = 0
            TracePrint "自动6次合作子程序判断了对战结束确定"
            Call MoveToFcn(515,535)
            Delay 100
            dm.LeftClick
        End If

        If 世界频道社交() = 1 Then
            Call MoveToFcn(620,586)
            Delay 50
            dm.LeftClick
            Delay 3000
        End If

        If 出现房间号 <> - 1  Then
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop

End Function

Sub 双开自动完成6次合作()
    If 双开自动完成6次合作Index = 0 Then
        Call 双开自动完成6次合作a()
    ElseIf 双开自动完成6次合作Index = 1 Then
        Call 双开自动完成6次合作b()
    End If
End Sub

Sub 双开自动完成6次寒冰()
    If 双开自动完成6次合作Index = 0 Then
        Call 双开自动完成6次寒冰a()
    ElseIf 双开自动完成6次合作Index = 1 Then
        Call 双开自动完成6次寒冰b()
    End If
End Sub



Function 双开自动完成6次合作b()
    Dim 好友房间号

    Do
        If 世界频道社交() = 1 Then
            Call MoveToFcn(620,586)
            Delay 50
            dm.LeftClick
            Delay 1000
        End If

        For 3
            Delay 500
            dm_ret = remoteFindPic("红叉.tif|红叉2.bmp", "69,73,962,528", 0.85, 1)
            If len(dm_ret) > 1 Then
                TracePrint "自动6次合作开局检查到红叉"
                Dim res
                res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
                Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0)),int(res(1)))
                Delay 100
                dm.LeftClick
                红叉i = 0
                TracePrint "关闭红叉"
                Exit For
            End If
        Next

        Do
            好友房间号 = Form1.InputBox74.Text
            If len(好友房间号) > 0 Then
                If 双开自动6次后转对战ck = 1 Then
                    If 好友房间号 = "转对战"  Then
                        Call 转对战()
                        Exit Function
                    End If
                End If
                好友房间号 = Int(好友房间号)
                If 好友房间号 > 999 Then
                    Exit Do
                Else
                    TracePrint "房间号不对，清空"
                    Form1.InputBox74.Text = ""
                End If
            End If
            Delay 2000
            TracePrint "等待房间号中"
        Loop

        If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
            TracePrint "拿到最终房间号：" & 好友房间号
            is合作次数 = 1
            For 4
                If 合作助战() = 1 Then
                    is合作次数 = 0
                    Exit For
                End If
                Delay 1000
            Next
            If is合作次数 = 0 Then
                Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                Delay 200
                dm.LeftClick
                Delay 1000

                For 5
					助战弹窗ret= 合作助战弹窗()
                    If len(助战弹窗ret) > 1 Then
                        //点击输入框
                        Call MoveToFcn(504,358)
                        Delay 100
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 500
                        Call 喊话输入(好友房间号)

                        //确定助战
                        Delay 1000
                        res = Split(助战弹窗ret, "|")
						Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                        Delay 50
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 100

                        For 8
                            Delay 300
                            If 加入房间失败() = 1 Then
                                好友房间号 = -1
                                Form1.InputBox74.Text = ""
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next
                        If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                            Delay 5000
                        End If

                        //关闭助战
                        Call 关闭助战()

                        is合作次数 = 0
                        Exit For
                    End If
                    Delay 500
                Next

            End If

            If is合作次数 = 1 Then
                Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                Delay 50
                dm.LeftClick
                For 5
                    Delay 500
                    If 合作未打完弹窗() = 1 Then
                        //点击与好友一起
                        For 3
                            Call MoveToFcn(337,516)
                            Delay 50
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 100
                        Next

                        Delay 2000

                        //确定加入
                        For 3
                            Call MoveToFcn(598,430)
                            Delay 50
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 100
                        Next


                        Delay 2000


                        //点击输入框
                        Call MoveToFcn(504,358)
                        Delay 100
                        dm.LeftClick



                        Delay 2000

                        Call 喊话输入(好友房间号)

                        //确定助战
                        Delay 1000
                        For 1
                            Call MoveToFcn(524,430)
                            Delay 50
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 100
                        Next

                        For 8
                            Delay 300
                            If 加入房间失败() = 1 Then
                                好友房间号 = -1
                                Form1.InputBox74.Text = ""
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next
                        If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                            Delay 2500
                        End If
                        //关闭助战
                        Call 关闭助战()
                        is合作次数 = -1
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next
            End If

            //合作看广告判断
            If is合作次数 = 1 Then
                Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                Delay 50
                dm.LeftClick

                For 5
                    Delay 500

                    dm_ret = remoteFindPic("合作看广告界面.tif", "416,406,462,450", 0.8, 1)
                    If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then
                        //点击看广告
                        Call MoveToFcn(522,432)
                        Delay 50
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 2000

                        dm_ret = remoteFindPic("合作看广告界面.tif", "416,406,462,450", 0.8, 1)
                        If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then
                            Call MoveToFcn(522,432)
                            Delay 50
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 2000
                        End If


                        Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                        Delay 100
                        dm.LeftClick

                        If 是否有免广告特权ck = 1 Then
                            For 30
                                Delay 1000
                                Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                                Delay 100
                                dm.LeftClick
                                If 主界面对战按钮() = 1 or len(返回主页面按钮()) > 0 Then
                                    Exit For
                                End If
                            Next
                        Else

                            广告卡r = 0
                            无广告可看r = 0
                            For 4
                                Delay 1000
                                If 广告卡r = 0 Then
                                    广告卡r= 广告卡()
                                End If

                                If 广告卡r = 2 Then
                                    Exit For
                                Else
                                    Delay 1000
                                    无广告可看r = 无广告可看()
                                    If 无广告可看r = 1 Then
                                        Exit For
                                    End If
                                End If

                            Next

                            Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                            Delay 100
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 100
                            dm.LeftClick
                            If 主界面对战按钮() = 1 or len(返回主页面按钮()) > 0 Then
                                广告卡r = 2
                            End If


                            If 广告卡r <> 2 Then
                                If 无广告可看r <> 1 Then
                                    Call 关闭广告()
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If

                        //点合作
                        Delay 3000
                        Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                        Delay 200
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 2000

                        For 3
                            Delay 1000
                            If 合作未打完弹窗() = 1 Then
                                //点击与好友一起
                                Call MoveToFcn(337,516)
                                Delay 200
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1500
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1500

                                //确定加入
                                Call MoveToFcn(598,430)
                                Delay 200
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1500
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1500


                                //点击输入框
                                Call MoveToFcn(504,358)
                                Delay 100
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 2000

                                Call 喊话输入(好友房间号)

                                //确定助战
                                Delay 1000
                                For 1
                                    Call MoveToFcn(524,430)
                                    Delay 50
                                    dm.LeftClick
                                    Delay 100
                                Next
                                For 8
                                    Delay 300
                                    If 加入房间失败() = 1 Then
                                        好友房间号 = -1
                                        Form1.InputBox74.Text = ""
                                        Exit For
                                    End If
                                Next
                                If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                                    Delay 2500
                                End If
                                //关闭助战
                                Call 关闭助战()

                                is合作次数 = -1
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next

                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next
            End If


        Else
            TracePrint "当前好友没房间号：" & 好友房间号
        End If

        If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
            好友房间号 = -1
            For 40
                Delay 1000
                If 判断当前是否在战场中() = 1 Then
                    当前在战斗 = 1
                    TracePrint "进入战场了"
                    Delay 50
                    //变量初始化
                    Call paramInit()
                    If getCard("幻精灵") = 0 Then
                        装备切换 = - 1
                    ElseIf 默认初始装备 = "无" Then
                        装备切换 = 1
                    End If
                    //合作标志 = 1
                    Call 合作标志Handle(1)
                    扩建控制 = 合作前期扩建允许次数
                    好友房间号 = 1
                    TracePrint "已进入好友房间，开始合作"
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next
        End If



        Delay 200
        If 对战结束确定() = 1 Then
            当前在战斗 = 0
            TracePrint "好友合作子程序判断了对战结束确定"
            Call MoveToFcn(515,535)
            Delay 100
            dm.LeftClick

        End If

        If 好友房间号 <> -1 Then
            Exit Do
        End If


    Loop

End Function

Function 双开自动完成6次合作a()

    Dim 出现房间号,好友房间号s, hyLen, hyi, 好友房间号1, 好友房间号2
    出现房间号 = 0
    Do
        If 世界频道社交() = 1 Then
            Call MoveToFcn(620,586)
            Delay 50
            dm.LeftClick
            Delay 1000
        End If

        For 3
            Delay 500
            dm_ret = remoteFindPic("红叉.tif|红叉2.bmp", "69,73,962,528", 0.85, 1)
            If len(dm_ret) > 1 Then
                TracePrint "自动6次合作开局检查到红叉"
                Dim res
                res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
                Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0)),int(res(1)))
                Delay 100
                dm.LeftClick
                红叉i = 0
                TracePrint "关闭红叉"
            End If
        Next

        If 1 > 0 Then
            is合作次数 = 1
            For 3
                If 合作助战() = 1 Then
                    is合作次数 = 0
                    Exit For
                End If
                Delay 1000
            Next
            If is合作次数 = 0 Then
                If 双开自动6次后转对战ck = 1 Then
                    Form1.InputBox74.Text ="转对战"
                    Call 转对战()
                    Exit Function
                Else
                    MessageBox "合作已打完，该号不能创建房间了"
                End If

                Exit Do
            End If

            If is合作次数 = 1 Then
                Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                Delay 50
                dm.LeftClick
                For 5
                    Delay 500
                    If 合作未打完弹窗() = 1 Then
                        //点击与好友一起
                        For 3
                            Call MoveToFcn(337,516)
                            Delay 50
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 100
                        Next
                        Delay 3000

                        //创建房间
                        Call MoveToFcn(418,431)
                        Delay 50
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 2000

                        出现房间号 = 1
                        is合作次数 = 0
                        Exit For
                    End If

                Next
            End If

            //合作看广告判断
            If is合作次数 = 1 Then
                Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                Delay 50
                dm.LeftClick

                For 5
                    Delay 600
                    dm_ret = remoteFindPic("合作看广告界面.tif", "416,406,462,450", 0.8, 1)
                    If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then
                        //点击看广告
                        Call MoveToFcn(522,432)
                        Delay 50
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 2000

                        dm_ret = remoteFindPic("合作看广告界面.tif", "416,406,462,450", 0.8, 1)
                        If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then
                            Call MoveToFcn(522,432)
                            Delay 50
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 2000
                        End If


                        Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                        Delay 100
                        dm.LeftClick

                        If 是否有免广告特权ck = 1 Then
                            For 30
                                Delay 1000
                                Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                                Delay 100
                                dm.LeftClick
                                If 主界面对战按钮() = 1 or len(返回主页面按钮()) > 0 Then
                                    Exit For
                                End If
                            Next
                        Else

                            广告卡r = 0
                            无广告可看r = 0
                            For 4
                                Delay 1000
                                If 广告卡r = 0 Then
                                    广告卡r= 广告卡()
                                End If

                                If 广告卡r = 2 Then
                                    Exit For
                                Else
                                    Delay 1000
                                    无广告可看r = 无广告可看()
                                    If 无广告可看r = 1 Then
                                        Exit For
                                    End If
                                End If

                            Next

                            Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                            Delay 100
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 100
                            dm.LeftClick
                            If 主界面对战按钮() = 1 or len(返回主页面按钮()) > 0 Then
                                广告卡r = 2
                            End If


                            If 广告卡r <> 2 Then
                                If 无广告可看r <> 1 Then
                                    Call 关闭广告()
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If

                        //点合作
                        Delay 3000
                        Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                        Delay 200
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 1000

                        For 5
                            Delay 500
                            If 合作未打完弹窗() = 1 Then
                                //点击与好友一起
                                For 3
                                    Call MoveToFcn(337,516)
                                    Delay 50
                                    dm.LeftClick
                                    Delay 100
                                Next
                                Delay 3000

                                //创建房间
                                Call MoveToFcn(418,431)
                                Delay 50
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 2000

                                出现房间号 = 1
                                Exit For
                            End If

                        Next

                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next
            End If
        End If



        If 出现房间号 > 0 Then

            For 5
                Delay 200
                好友房间号 = remoteOcr("484, 344, 560, 374", 0, 0)
                好友房间号1 = remoteOcr("480,341,540,368", 0, 0)
                好友房间号2 = remoteOcr("481,344,546,366", 0, 0)
                好友房间号s = Array(好友房间号, 好友房间号1, 好友房间号2)
				hyLen = UBound(好友房间号s)
				For hyi = 0 To hyLen
					好友房间号 = 好友房间号s(hyi)
					 TracePrint "识别到房间号为：" & 好友房间号
                	If len(好友房间号) <= 0 Then
                    	//执行创建房间
                    	出现房间号 = 0
                	Else
                    	If Int(好友房间号) < 1000 Then
                    		好友房间号 = 0
                    	Else
                    		Form1.InputBox74.Text = 好友房间号
                    		出现房间号 = 1
                    		Delay 2000
                    		dm_ret = 社交频道入口(0)
                    		If len (dm_ret) > 1 Then
                        		res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
								Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                        		Delay 50
                        		dm.LeftClick
                    		End If
                    		TracePrint "循环拿到房间号，退出：" & 好友房间号
                    		Exit For
                    		End If
                	End If

				Next


                If Int(好友房间号) > 999 Then
                	Exit For
                End If
            Next

            If 出现房间号 > 0 Then
                出现房间号 = -1
                For 180
                    Delay 1000
                    TracePrint "等待进入战场中..."
                    If 判断当前是否在战场中() = 1 Then
                        当前在战斗 = 1
                        TracePrint "进入战场了"
                        Delay 50
                        //变量初始化
                        Call paramInit()
                        If getCard("幻精灵") = 0 Then
                            装备切换 = - 1
                        ElseIf 默认初始装备 = "无" Then
                            装备切换 = 1
                        End If
                        //合作标志 = 1
                        Call 合作标志Handle(1)
                        扩建控制 = 合作前期扩建允许次数
                        TracePrint "已进入好友房间，开始合作"
                        出现房间号 = 1
                        Form1.InputBox74.Text = ""
                        Exit For
                    End If

                    If len(Form1.InputBox74.Text) < 2 Then
                        TracePrint "检查到房间号无效，重新创建"
                        出现房间号 = - 1
                        Delay 2000
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next
            End If
        End If

        Delay 200
        If 对战结束确定() = 1 Then
            当前在战斗 = 0
            TracePrint "自动6次合作子程序判断了对战结束确定"
            Call MoveToFcn(515,535)
            Delay 100
            dm.LeftClick
        End If

        If 世界频道社交() = 1 Then
            Call MoveToFcn(620,586)
            Delay 50
            dm.LeftClick
            Delay 3000
        End If

        If 出现房间号 <> - 1  Then
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop

End Function

Function 创建房间发送给好友()

    Dim 出现房间号
    出现房间号 = 0
    Do
        If 世界频道社交() = 1 Then
            Call MoveToFcn(620,586)
            Delay 50
            dm.LeftClick
            Delay 1000
        End If

        For 3
            Delay 500
            dm_ret = remoteFindPic("红叉.tif|红叉2.bmp", "69,73,962,528", 0.85, 1)
            If len(dm_ret) > 1 Then
                TracePrint "自动6次合作开局检查到红叉"
                Dim res
                res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
                Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0)),int(res(1)))
                Delay 100
                dm.LeftClick
                红叉i = 0
                TracePrint "关闭红叉"
            End If
        Next

        If 1 = 1 Then
            is合作次数 = 1
            For 3
                If 合作助战() = 1 Then
                    is合作次数 = 0
                    Exit For
                End If
                Delay 1000
            Next
            If is合作次数 = 0 Then
                If 双开自动6次后转对战ck = 1 Then
                    Form1.InputBox74.Text ="转对战"
                    Call 转对战()
                    Exit Function
                Else
                    MessageBox "合作已打完，该号不能创建房间了"
                End If
                Exit Do
            End If

            If is合作次数 = 1 Then
                Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                Delay 50
                dm.LeftClick
                For 5
                    Delay 500
                    If 合作未打完弹窗() = 1 Then
                        //点击与好友一起
                        For 3
                            Call MoveToFcn(337,516)
                            Delay 50
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 100
                        Next
                        Delay 3000

                        //创建房间
                        Call MoveToFcn(418,431)
                        Delay 50
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 2000

                        出现房间号 = 1
                        is合作次数 = 0
                        Exit For
                    End If

                Next
            End If

            //合作看广告判断
            If is合作次数 = 1 Then
                Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                Delay 50
                dm.LeftClick

                For 5
                    Delay 600
                    dm_ret = remoteFindPic("合作看广告界面.tif", "416,406,462,450", 0.8, 1)
                    If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then
                        //点击看广告
                        Call MoveToFcn(522,432)
                        Delay 50
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 2000

                        dm_ret = remoteFindPic("合作看广告界面.tif", "416,406,462,450", 0.8, 1)
                        If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then
                            Call MoveToFcn(522,432)
                            Delay 50
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 2000
                        End If


                        Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                        Delay 100
                        dm.LeftClick

                        If 是否有免广告特权ck = 1 Then
                            For 30
                                Delay 1000
                                Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                                Delay 100
                                dm.LeftClick
                                If 主界面对战按钮() = 1 or len(返回主页面按钮()) > 0 Then
                                    Exit For
                                End If
                            Next
                        Else

                            广告卡r = 0
                            无广告可看r = 0
                            For 4
                                Delay 1000
                                If 广告卡r = 0 Then
                                    广告卡r= 广告卡()
                                End If

                                If 广告卡r = 2 Then
                                    Exit For
                                Else
                                    Delay 1000
                                    无广告可看r = 无广告可看()
                                    If 无广告可看r = 1 Then
                                        Exit For
                                    End If
                                End If

                            Next

                            Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                            Delay 100
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 100
                            dm.LeftClick
                            If 主界面对战按钮() = 1 or len(返回主页面按钮()) > 0 Then
                                广告卡r = 2
                            End If


                            If 广告卡r <> 2 Then
                                If 无广告可看r <> 1 Then
                                    Call 关闭广告()
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If

                        //点合作
                        Delay 3000
                        Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                        Delay 200
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 1000

                        For 5
                            Delay 500
                            If 合作未打完弹窗() = 1 Then
                                //点击与好友一起
                                For 3
                                    Call MoveToFcn(337,516)
                                    Delay 50
                                    dm.LeftClick
                                    Delay 100
                                Next
                                Delay 3000

                                //创建房间
                                Call MoveToFcn(418,431)
                                Delay 50
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 2000

                                出现房间号 = 1
                                Exit For
                            End If

                        Next

                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next
            End If
        End If



        If 出现房间号 > 0 Then

            For 10
                Delay 200
                好友房间号 = remoteOcr("484, 344, 560, 374", 0, 0)
                TracePrint "识别到房间号为：" & 好友房间号
                If len(好友房间号) <= 0 Then
                    //执行创建房间
                    出现房间号 = 0
                Else
                    If Int(好友房间号) < 1000 Then
                    	好友房间号 = 0 & 好友房间号
                    End If
                    Form1.InputBox74.Text = 好友房间号
                    出现房间号 = 1
                    Delay 2000
                    dm_ret = 社交频道入口(0)
                    If len (dm_ret) > 1 Then
                      	res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
						Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                        Delay 50
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 2000
                    End If

                    If 世界频道社交() = 1  Then
                        Delay 2000
                        //点击好友
                        For 3
                            Call MoveToFcn(785,65)
                            Delay 200
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 1000
                        Next

                        //点击输入框
                        Delay 200
                        Call MoveToFcn(817,580)
                        Delay 200
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 200
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Call 喊话输入(好友房间号)

                        //发送
                        Delay 200
                        Call MoveToFcn(968,584)
                        Delay 200
                        dm.LeftClick
                         Call MoveToFcn(986,583)
                        Delay 500
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 2000
                        Exit For
                    End If
                End If
            Next

            If 出现房间号 > 0 Then
                出现房间号 = -1
                For 180
                    Delay 1000
                    TracePrint "等待进入战场中..."
                    If 判断当前是否在战场中() = 1 Then
                        当前在战斗 = 1
                        TracePrint "进入战场了"
                        Delay 50
                        //变量初始化
                        Call paramInit()
                        If getCard("幻精灵") = 0 Then
                            装备切换 = - 1
                        ElseIf 默认初始装备 = "无" Then
                            装备切换 = 1
                        End If
                        //合作标志 = 1
                        Call 合作标志Handle(1)
                        扩建控制 = 合作前期扩建允许次数
                        TracePrint "已进入好友房间，开始合作"
                        出现房间号 = 1
                        Form1.InputBox74.Text = ""
                        Exit For
                    End If

                    If len(Form1.InputBox74.Text) < 2 Then
                        TracePrint "检查到房间号无效，重新创建"
                        出现房间号 = - 1
                        Delay 2000
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next
            End If
        End If

        Delay 200
        If 对战结束确定() = 1 Then
            当前在战斗 = 0
            TracePrint "自动6次合作子程序判断了对战结束确定"
            Call MoveToFcn(515,535)
            Delay 100
            dm.LeftClick
        End If

        If 世界频道社交() = 1 Then
            Call MoveToFcn(620,586)
            Delay 50
            dm.LeftClick
            Delay 3000
        End If

        If 出现房间号 <> - 1  Then
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop

End Function

Function 寒冰创建房间发送给好友()
    Dim 好友房间号, 寒冰界面1, 寒冰界面2,  与好友一起, 创建房间,出现房间号
    出现房间号 = 0
    Do
        If 进入寒冰主界面() = 1 Then

            For 100
                //创建房间
                Delay 1000

                //单击冰堡挑战
                Call MoveToFcn(840,567)
                Delay 50
                dm.LeftClick
                Delay 1500

                与好友一起 = remoteFindPic("与好友一起.bmp", "286,462,395,515", 0.83, 1)
                If len(与好友一起) > 0 Then

					res = Split(与好友一起, "|")
					Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                    Delay 50
                    dm.LeftClick
                    Delay 1500
                End If

                创建房间 = remoteFindPic("创建房间.bmp", "316,368,540,519", 0.80, 1)
                If len(创建房间) > 0 Then
                   res = Split(创建房间, "|")
					Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                    Delay 50
                    dm.LeftClick
                    Delay 1500
                    出现房间号 = 1
                End If

                Exit For
            Next
        End If



        If 出现房间号 > 0 Then

            For 10
                Delay 200
                好友房间号 = remoteOcr("441,337,606,373", 0, 0)
                TracePrint "识别到房间号为：" & 好友房间号
                If len(好友房间号) <= 0 Then
                    //执行创建房间
                    出现房间号 = 0
                Else
                    If Int(好友房间号) > 999 Then
                        Form1.InputBox74.Text = 好友房间号
                        出现房间号 = 1
                        Delay 2000
                        dm_ret = 社交频道入口(0)
                        If len (dm_ret) > 1 Then
                            res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
							Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                            Delay 50
                            dm.LeftClick
                        End If

                        If 世界频道社交() = 1  Then
                            Delay 2000
                            //点击好友
                            For 3
                                Call MoveToFcn(704,65)
                                Delay 200
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1000
                            Next

                            //点击输入框
                            Delay 200
                            Call MoveToFcn(817,580)
                            Delay 200
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 200
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Call 喊话输入(好友房间号)

                            //发送
                            Delay 200
                            Call MoveToFcn(968,584)
                            Delay 200
                            dm.LeftClick
                             Call MoveToFcn(986,583)
                            Delay 500
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 2000
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Else
                        出现房间号 = 0
                    End If
                End If
            Next

            If 出现房间号 > 0 Then
                出现房间号 = -1
                For 180
                    Delay 1000
                    TracePrint "等待进入战场中..."
                    If 判断当前是否在战场中() = 1 Then
                        当前在战斗 = 1
                        TracePrint "进入战场了"
                        Delay 50
                        //变量初始化
                        Call paramInit()
                        If getCard("幻精灵") = 0 Then
                            装备切换 = - 1
                        ElseIf 默认初始装备 = "无" Then
                            装备切换 = 1
                        End If
                        //合作标志 = 1
                        Call 合作标志Handle(1)
                        扩建控制 = 合作前期扩建允许次数
                        TracePrint "已进入好友房间，开始合作"
                        出现房间号 = 1
                        Form1.InputBox74.Text = ""
                        Exit For
                    End If

                    If len(Form1.InputBox74.Text) < 2 Then
                        TracePrint "检查到房间号无效，重新创建"
                        出现房间号 = - 1
                        Delay 2000
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next
            End If
        End If

        Delay 200
        If 对战结束确定() = 1 Then
            当前在战斗 = 0
            TracePrint "自动6次合作子程序判断了对战结束确定"
            Call MoveToFcn(515,535)
            Delay 100
            dm.LeftClick
        End If

        If 世界频道社交() = 1 Then
            Call MoveToFcn(620,586)
            Delay 50
            dm.LeftClick
            Delay 3000
        End If

        If 出现房间号 <> - 1  Then
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop

End Function

Function 关闭好友头像()
    关闭好友头像 = 0
    dm_ret = remoteFindPic("好友头像叉1.bmp|好友头像叉2.bmp", "599,93,732,116", 0.83, 1)
    If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then
        Dim res
        res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
        Call MoveToFcn(int(res(0) + 3), int(res(1)) + 3)
        Delay 50
        dm.LeftClick
        TracePrint "关闭好友头像成功"
        关闭好友头像 = 1
    End If
End Function
Function 好友房间合作(tag)
    If 好友房间自动识别ck = 1 Then
        TracePrint "好友合作来了"
        If tag = 1 Then
            Goto 好友房间直接执行
        End If
        //聊天窗口
        If 1 = 1 Then
            Do
                Delay 60
                dm_ret = 社交频道入口(0)
                If len (dm_ret) > 1 Then
                    res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
					Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                    Delay 200
                    dm.LeftClick
                    Delay 1500

                    If 世界频道社交() = 1 Then
                        //点击好友
                        Call MoveToFcn(785,65)
                        Delay 200
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 1500
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 500



                        Delay 100
                        dm_ret = remoteFindStr("728,316,888,356","暂无")
    					If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then

                            Do
                                Delay 200
                                dm_ret = remoteFindStr("728,316,888,356","暂无")
    							If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then


                                Else
                                    Exit Do
                                End If

                                TracePrint "等待好友中....."
                                Delay 8000

                            Loop

                        End If

                        If tag = 1 Then
                            Rem 好友房间直接执行
                            //点击好友
                            Call MoveToFcn(785,65)
                            Delay 200
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 1500
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 500
                        End If



                        //点击第一个好友头像
                        Call MoveToFcn(643,136)
                        Delay 500
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Delay 500
                        dm.LeftClick

                        好友房间号 = 获取好友房间号()
                        TracePrint "得到最终房间号： " & 好友房间号

                        Delay 500
                        If tag = 1 Then
                            Call 关闭好友头像()
                        Else
                            //点击好友头像的叉
                            Call 关闭好友头像()
                            Delay 100
                            dm.LeftClick
                        End If


                        If 好友房间自动识别ck = 1 and 好友房间号 = 0 Then
                            If 社交喊话ck = 1 or 世界喊话ck = 1 Then
                                If 好友消息通知() = 1 Then
                                    Do	While 好友房间号 = 0
                                        //点击第一个好友头像
                                        Call MoveToFcn(643,136)
                                        Delay 500
                                        dm.LeftClick
                                        Delay 500
                                        dm.LeftClick
                                        好友房间号 = 获取好友房间号()
                                        Delay 500
                                        //点击好友头像的叉
                                        If tag = 1 Then
                                            Call 关闭好友头像()
                                        Else
                                            //点击好友头像的叉
                                            Call 关闭好友头像()
                                            Delay 100
                                            dm.LeftClick
                                        End If
                                        If 好友消息通知() = 0 Then
                                            Exit Do
                                        End If
                                    Loop

                                End If
                            End If
                        End If

                        Delay 200
                        Call 关闭通讯()

                        If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                            TracePrint "拿到最终房间号：" & 好友房间号
                            //打开助战
                            is合作次数 = 1
                            For 2
                                If 合作助战() = 1 Then
                                    is合作次数 = 0
                                    Exit For
                                End If
                                Delay 500
                                Call 关闭通讯()
                            Next
                            If is合作次数 = 0 Then

                                Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                                Delay 200
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1000
                                is合作次数 = 1
                                For 5
									助战弹窗ret= 合作助战弹窗()
                                    If len(助战弹窗ret) > 1 Then
                                        TracePrint "助战合作"
                                        //点击输入框
                                        Call MoveToFcn(504,358)
                                        Delay 100
                                        dm.LeftClick
                                        Delay 1000
                                        Call 喊话输入(好友房间号)

                                        //确定助战
                                        Delay 1000
                                        For 1
                                            res = Split(助战弹窗ret, "|")
											Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                                            Delay 50
                                            dm.LeftClick
                                            Delay 100
                                        Next
                                        For 8
                                            Delay 300
                                            If 加入房间失败() = 1 Then
                                                好友房间号 = 0
                                                Exit For
                                            End If
                                        Next
                                        If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                                            Delay 2000
                                        End If
                                        //关闭助战
                                        Call 关闭助战()

                                        is合作次数 = 0
                                        Exit For
                                    End If
                                    Delay 100
                                Next

                                If 判断当前是否在战场中() = 1 and 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                                    当前在战斗 = 1
                                    TracePrint "进入好友战场了"
                                    Delay 100
                                    //变量初始化
                                    Call paramInit()
                                    If getCard("幻精灵") = 0 Then
                                        装备切换 = - 1
                                    ElseIf 默认初始装备 = "无" Then
                                        装备切换 = 1
                                    End If
                                    //合作标志 = 1
                                    Call 合作标志Handle(1)
                                    扩建控制 = 合作前期扩建允许次数
                                    TracePrint "已进入好友房间，开始合作"
                                    Exit Do
                                End If

                            End If



                            If is合作次数 = 1 Then
                                Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                                Delay 100
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1000

                                For 3
                                    Delay 500
                                    If 合作未打完弹窗() = 1 Then
                                        //点击与好友一起
                                        Call MoveToFcn(337,516)
                                        Delay 200
                                        dm.LeftClick
                                        Delay 1000
                                        dm.LeftClick
                                        Delay 1000

                                        //确定加入
                                        Call MoveToFcn(598,430)
                                        Delay 200
                                        dm.LeftClick
                                        Delay 1000
                                        dm.LeftClick
                                        Delay 1000


                                        //点击输入框
                                        Call MoveToFcn(504,358)
                                        Delay 100
                                        dm.LeftClick
                                        Delay 1000


                                        Call 喊话输入(好友房间号)

                                        //确定助战
                                        Delay 1000
                                        For 1
                                            Call MoveToFcn(524,430)
                                            Delay 50
                                            dm.LeftClick
                                            Delay 100
                                        Next
                                        For 8
                                            Delay 300
                                            If 加入房间失败() = 1 Then
                                                好友房间号 = 0
                                                Exit For
                                            End If
                                        Next
                                        If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                                            Delay 2500
                                        End If
                                        //关闭助战
                                        Call 关闭助战()

                                        is合作次数 = -1
                                        Exit For
                                    End If
                                Next
                            End If

                            //合作看广告判断
                            If is合作次数 = 1 Then
                                Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                                Delay 100
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1000

                                For 3
                                    Delay 500
                                    dm_ret = dm.FindColorBlock(430,421,448,438,"ffffff-050505|f89738-050505",0.85,170,18,17,intX,intY)
                                    If dm_ret = 1 Then
                                        //点击看广告
                                        Call MoveToFcn(522,432)
                                        Delay 50
                                        dm.LeftClick
                                        Delay 1000

                                        dm_ret = remoteFindPic("合作看广告界面.tif", "416,406,462,450", 0.8, 1)
                                        If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then
                                            Call MoveToFcn(522,432)
                                            Delay 50
                                            dm.LeftClick
                                            Delay 1000
                                        End If


                                        Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                                        Delay 100
                                        dm.LeftClick

                                        If 是否有免广告特权ck = 1 Then
                                            For 30
                                                Delay 1000
                                                Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                                                Delay 100
                                                dm.LeftClick
                                                If 主界面对战按钮() = 1 or len(返回主页面按钮()) > 0 Then

                                                    Exit For
                                                End If
                                            Next
                                        Else

                                            广告卡r = 0
                                            无广告可看r = 0
                                            For 4
                                                Delay 1000
                                                If 广告卡r = 0 Then
                                                    广告卡r= 广告卡()
                                                End If

                                                If 广告卡r = 2 Then
                                                    Exit For
                                                Else
                                                    Delay 1000
                                                    无广告可看r = 无广告可看()
                                                    If 无广告可看r = 1 Then
                                                        Exit For
                                                    End If
                                                End If

                                            Next

                                            Call MoveToFcn(936,44)
                                            Delay 100
                                            dm.LeftClick
                                            Delay 100
                                            dm.LeftClick
                                            If 主界面对战按钮() = 1 or len(返回主页面按钮()) > 0  Then
                                                广告卡r = 2
                                            End If


                                            If 广告卡r <> 2 Then
                                                If 无广告可看r <> 1 Then
                                                    Call 关闭广告()
                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                        //点合作
                                        Delay 1000
                                        Call MoveToFcn(629,571)
                                        Delay 200
                                        dm.LeftClick
                                        Delay 1000

                                        For 3
                                            Delay 1000
                                            //dm_ret = dm.FindPic(296,463,395,523, "与好友一起1.bmp|与好友一起2.bmp|与好友一起3.bmp", "30", 0.80, 0, intX, intY)
                                            If 合作未打完弹窗() = 1 Then
                                                //点击与好友一起
                                                Call MoveToFcn(337,516)
                                                Delay 200
                                                dm.LeftClick
                                                Delay 1500
                                                dm.LeftClick
                                                Delay 1000

                                                //确定加入
                                                Call MoveToFcn(598,430)
                                                Delay 200
                                                dm.LeftClick
                                                Delay 1500
                                                dm.LeftClick
                                                Delay 1000


                                                //点击输入框
                                                Call MoveToFcn(504,358)
                                                Delay 100
                                                dm.LeftClick
                                                Delay 2000

                                                Call 喊话输入(好友房间号)

                                                //确定助战
                                                Delay 1000
                                                For 1
                                                    Call MoveToFcn(524,430)
                                                    Delay 50
                                                    dm.LeftClick
                                                    Delay 100
                                                Next
                                                For 8
                                                    Delay 300
                                                    If 加入房间失败() = 1 Then
                                                        好友房间号 = 0
                                                        Exit For
                                                    End If
                                                Next
                                                If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                                                    Delay 2500
                                                End If
                                                //关闭助战
                                                Call 关闭助战()

                                                is合作次数 = -1
                                                Exit For
                                            End If
                                        Next


                                        Exit For
                                    End If
                                Next
                            End If


                        Else
                            TracePrint "当前好友没房间号：" & 好友房间号
                        End If
                    Else
                        TracePrint "没找到社交"
                        Delay 500
                        Call 关闭通讯()
                    End If
                Else
                    Call 关闭通讯()
                End If

                If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                    //变量初始化
                    Call paramInit()
                    If getCard("幻精灵") = 0 Then
                        装备切换 = - 1
                    ElseIf 默认初始装备 = "无" Then
                        装备切换 = 1
                    End If
                    //合作标志 = 1
                    Call 合作标志Handle(1)
                    扩建控制 = 合作前期扩建允许次数
                    Dim count
                    count = 0
                    For 40
                        Delay 1000
                        If 判断当前是否在战场中() = 1 Then
                            TracePrint "进入战场了"
                            当前在战斗 = 1
                            TracePrint "已进入好友房间，开始合作"
                            Exit Do
                        End If
                        If 对战结束确定() = 1 Then
                            当前在战斗 = 0
                            TracePrint "好友合作子程序判断了对战结束确定"
                            Call MoveToFcn(515,535)
                            Delay 100
                            dm.LeftClick
                            好友房间号 = 0
                            Exit For
                        End If

                        If 判断当前是否在主界面() = 1 and count > 2 Then
                            当前在战斗 = 0
                            TracePrint "判断当前是否在主界面"
                            好友房间号 = 0
                            Exit For
                        End If
       					count = count + 1
                    Next

                    Delay 100
                    If 对战结束确定() = 1 Then
                        当前在战斗 = 0
                        TracePrint "好友合作子程序判断了对战结束确定"
                        Call MoveToFcn(515,535)
                        Delay 100
                        dm.LeftClick
                        好友房间号 = 0
                    End If
                End If
                TracePrint "当前是否从社交喊话进入：" & tag
                If tag = 1 Then
                    TracePrint "好友房间号:" & 好友房间号
                    If 好友房间号 = 0 Then
                        TracePrint "进入社交喊话..."
                        For 5
                            Delay 500
                            dm_ret = 社交频道入口(0)
                            If len (dm_ret) > 1 Then
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next
                        Call 世界社交喊话()
                    End If
                    Exit Do
                End If
            Loop
        End If
    End If
End Function

Function 进入寒冰主界面()
    进入寒冰主界面 = 0
    Dim  寒冰界面1,寒冰界面2, 活动, res
    Do
        If 世界频道社交() = 1 Then
            Call MoveToFcn(620,586)
            Delay 50
            dm.LeftClick
            Delay 1000
        End If

        For 3
            Delay 500
            dm_ret = remoteFindPic("红叉.tif|红叉2.bmp", "69,73,962,528", 0.85, 1)
            If len(dm_ret) > 1 Then
                TracePrint "自动6次合作开局检查到红叉"
                res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
                Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0)),int(res(1)))
                Delay 100
                dm.LeftClick
                红叉i = 0
                TracePrint "关闭红叉"
                Exit For
            End If
        Next


        寒冰界面2 = remoteFindPic("寒冰堡.bmp", "470,126,502,157", 0.75, 1)
        If len(寒冰界面2) > 0 Then
            进入寒冰主界面 = 1
            Exit Do
        Else
            If 世界频道社交() = 1 Then
                Call MoveToFcn(620,586)
                Delay 50
                dm.LeftClick
                Delay 1000
            End If

            For 3
                Delay 500
                dm_ret = remoteFindPic("红叉.tif|红叉2.bmp", "69,73,962,528", 0.85, 1)
                If len(dm_ret) > 1 Then
                    TracePrint "自动6次合作开局检查到红叉"
                    res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
                    Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0)),int(res(1)))
                    Delay 100
                    dm.LeftClick
                    红叉i = 0
                    TracePrint "关闭红叉"
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next

            Call 返回关闭()

            活动 = remoteFindPic("活动.bmp", "31,362,93,408", 0.83, 1)
            If len(活动) > 0 Then
                Call MoveToFcn(64,406)
                Delay 50
                dm.LeftClick
                Delay 1000

                For 3
                    Call 鼠标向左滑动()
                Next

                For 10
                    Delay 2000
                    寒冰界面1 = remoteFindPic("寒冰.bmp", "17,470,1016,511", 0.83, 1)
                    If len(寒冰界面1) > 0 Then
                        TracePrint "寒冰界面1:" & 寒冰界面1
                        res = Split(寒冰界面1, "|")
                        Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+136,int(res(1))-49)
                        Delay 100
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Exit For
                    Else
                        Call 鼠标向左滑动()
                    End If
                Next

            End If

        End If
        Delay 2000
    Loop
End Function


Function 好友房间寒冰()
    TracePrint "好友寒冰来了"
    Dim 好友房间号, res, 寒冰社交好友
    好友房间号 = -1
    Do

        If 进入寒冰主界面() = 1 Then
        	dm_ret = 社交频道入口("929,453,1001,513")
            If len (dm_ret) > 1 Then
                res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
				Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                Delay 200
                dm.LeftClick
                Delay 2000
            End If


            寒冰社交好友 = remoteFindPic("寒冰社交好友.bmp", "666,48,749,84", 0.83, 1)
            If len(寒冰社交好友) > 0 Then
                //点击好友
                Call MoveToFcn(705,63)
                Delay 200
                dm.LeftClick
                Delay 1500
                dm.LeftClick
                Delay 500


                Delay 200
                dm_ret = remoteFindStr("728,316,888,356","暂无")
    			If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then

                    Do

                        Delay 200
                        dm_ret = remoteFindStr("728,316,888,356","暂无")
    					If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then


                        Else
                            Exit Do
                        End If
                        TracePrint "等待好友中....."
                        Delay 8000

                    Loop

                End If


                //点击第一个好友头像
                Call MoveToFcn(643,136)
                Delay 500
                dm.LeftClick
                Delay 500
                dm.LeftClick

                好友房间号 = 获取好友房间号()
                TracePrint "得到最终房间号： " & 好友房间号

                Delay 1000
                Call 关闭好友头像()
                Delay 1000
                Call 关闭通讯()

                If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                    TracePrint "拿到最终房间号：" & 好友房间号
                    //打开助战
                    is合作次数 = 1
                    //判断寒冰是否有次数
                    If is合作次数 = 1 Then

                        For 100
                            //加入房间......
                            Delay 1000
                            Call 关闭通讯()
                            //单击冰堡挑战
                            Call MoveToFcn(840,567)
                            Delay 50
                            dm.LeftClick
                            Delay 2000

                            TracePrint "识别寒冰未打完助战"
                			寒冰未打完助战 = remoteFindPic("寒冰未打完助战.bmp", "353,443,433,486", 0.75, 1)
                			If len(寒冰未打完助战) > 0 Then
                    			TracePrint "识别寒冰未打完助战成功"
                    			res = Split(寒冰未打完助战, "|")
								Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                                Delay 50
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1500
                            End If

                            与好友一起 = remoteFindPic("与好友一起.bmp", "286,462,395,515", 0.83, 1)
                            If len(与好友一起) > 0 Then
                                res = Split(与好友一起, "|")
								Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                                Delay 50
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1500
                            End If

                              与好友一起 = remoteFindPic("与好友一起.bmp", "286,462,395,515", 0.75, 1)
                			  If len(与好友一起) > 0 Then
                    			res = Split(与好友一起, "|")
								Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                                Delay 50
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1500
                                出现房间号 = 1
                            End If

                            寒冰购买助战 = remoteFindPic("寒冰购买助战.bmp", "341,442,443,488", 0.80, 1)
                            If len(寒冰购买助战) > 0 Then
                                res = Split(寒冰购买助战, "|")
                                Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0)),int(res(1)))
                                Delay 50
                                dm.LeftClick
                                Delay 1500
                                出现房间号 = 1
                            End If

                            For 5
                                加入 = remoteFindPic("加入.bmp|合作寒冰助战.bmp", "491,416,547,452", 0.75, 1)
                                If len(加入) > 0  Then
                                    //点击输入框
                                    Call MoveToFcn(504,358)
                                    Delay 100
                                    dm.LeftClick
                                    Call 喊话输入(好友房间号)

                                   //确定助战
                        			Delay 1000
                        			res = Split(加入, "|")
									Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
                                    Delay 50
                                    dm.LeftClick
                                    Delay 100

                                    For 8
                                        Delay 300
                                        If 加入房间失败() = 1 Then
                                            好友房间号 = -1
                                            Exit For
                                        End If
                                    Next
                                    If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                                        Delay 5000
                                    End If

                                    //关闭助战
                                    Call 关闭助战()

                                    is合作次数 = 0
                                    Exit For
                                End If
                                Delay 500
                            Next

                            Exit For
                        Next
                    End If
                Else
                    好友房间号 = -1
                End If

            End If

            If 好友房间号 > 0 Then
                好友房间号 = -1
                For 40
                    Delay 1000
                    If 判断当前是否在战场中() = 1 Then
                        当前在战斗 = 1
                        TracePrint "进入战场了"
                        Delay 50
                        //变量初始化
                        Call paramInit()
                        If getCard("幻精灵") = 0 Then
                            装备切换 = - 1
                        ElseIf 默认初始装备 = "无" Then
                            装备切换 = 1
                        End If
                        //合作标志 = 1
                        Call 合作标志Handle(1)
                        扩建控制 = 合作前期扩建允许次数
                        好友房间号 = 1
                        TracePrint "已进入好友房间，开始合作"
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next
            End If
        End If

        Delay 200
        If 对战结束确定() = 1 Then
            当前在战斗 = 0
            TracePrint "好友合作子程序判断了对战结束确定"
            Call MoveToFcn(515,535)
            Delay 100
            dm.LeftClick

        End If

        If 好友房间号 <> -1 Then
            Exit Do
        End If

    Loop

End Function

Function get好友房间号Ocr(w)
    get好友房间号Ocr = ""
    If w = 1 Then
        get好友房间号Ocr = remoteOcr("708,235,982,288", 0,1)
    ElseIf w = 2 Then
        get好友房间号Ocr = remoteOcr("707,340,948,388", 0,1)
    ElseIf w = 3 Then
    	get好友房间号Ocr = remoteOcr("706,442,949,492", 0, 1)
    End If

  	If Int(get好友房间号Ocr) < 1000 and Int(get好友房间号Ocr) > 0 Then
  		TracePrint "get好友房间号Ocr 前面是零的被舍弃了，前面加零"
  		get好友房间号Ocr = 0 & get好友房间号Ocr
  	ElseIf get好友房间号Ocr < 0 Then
  		get好友房间号Ocr = ""
  	End If

End Function

Function 获取好友房间号()
	Dim 房间号
    获取好友房间号 = 0
    Delay 100
    房间号 = get好友房间号Ocr(1)
    TracePrint "房间号1：：：" & 房间号
    If Len(房间号) > 0 Then
        获取好友房间号 = 房间号
		Exit Function
    End If

     Delay 100
    房间号 = get好友房间号Ocr(2)
    TracePrint "房间号2：：：" & 房间号
    If Len(房间号) > 0 Then
        获取好友房间号 = 房间号
		Exit Function
    End If

     Delay 100
    房间号 = get好友房间号Ocr(3)
    TracePrint "房间号3：：：" & 房间号
    If Len(房间号) > 0 Then
        获取好友房间号 = 房间号
		Exit Function
    End If

End Function

Function 好友消息通知()
    好友消息通知 = 0
    dm_ret = remoteFindPic("领取宝箱提示.bmp", "809,45,839,71", 0.64, 1)
    If len(dm_ret) > 0 Then
        好友消息通知 = 1
    End If
End Function

Sub 世界社交喊话()
    Delay 100
    TracePrint "进入世界社交喊话"
    dm_ret = 社交频道入口(0)
    If len (dm_ret) > 1 Then
        res = Split(dm_ret, "|")
		Call MoveToFcn(Int(res(0))+5, Int(res(1))+5)
        Delay 200
        dm.LeftClick
        Delay 1000
        dm.LeftClick

        If 世界频道社交() = 1 Then
            TracePrint "世界社交喊话-扩展次数:" & 扩展次数
            If 扩展次数 > 0 Then
                TracePrint "社交好友合作前，清除好友消息"
                Delay 1500
                //点击好友
                For 3
                    Call MoveToFcn(785,65)
                    Delay 100
                    dm.LeftClick
                    Delay 50
                Next
                Delay 1500
                For 200
                    Delay 100
                    If 关闭好友头像() = 1 Then
                        Delay 1000
                    Else
                        TracePrint "没有消息，就退出清除循环"
                        扩展次数 = 0
                        //点击社交
                        Delay 1000
                        Call MoveToFcn(627,67)
                        Delay 100
                        dm.LeftClick
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next
            End If


            If 好友消息通知() = 1 Then
                TracePrint "世界社交喊话看到有消息，直接进入好友房间识别"
                Call 好友房间合作(1)
                Exit Sub
            End If

            Dim wxNowTime, 社交世界喊话时间间隔
            社交世界喊话时间间隔 = Int(社交世界喊话间隔text)
            If 社交喊话ck = 1 and 世界喊话ck = 1 Then
                Do While 世界喊话统计 < 501
                    wxNowTime = Now
                    Call 社交喊话do()
                    Delay 5000
                    Call 世界喊话do()
                    Do
                        If 计时器(wxNowTime) > 社交世界喊话时间间隔 Then
                            Exit Do
                        End If

                        If 好友消息通知() = 1 Then
                            Exit Do
                        End If
                        Delay 1000
                    Loop

                    If 好友消息通知() = 1 Then
                        Exit Do
                    End If
                Loop

            ElseIf 社交喊话ck = 1 Then
                Do While 社交喊话统计 < 101
                    wxNowTime = Now
                    Call 社交喊话do()
                    Do
                        If 计时器(wxNowTime) > 社交世界喊话时间间隔 Then
                            Exit Do
                        End If

                        If 好友消息通知() = 1 Then
                            Exit Do
                        End If
                        Delay 1000
                    Loop

                    If 好友消息通知() = 1 Then
                        Exit Do
                    End If
                Loop
            ElseIf 世界喊话ck = 1 Then
                Do While 世界喊话统计 < 501
                    wxNowTime = Now
                    Call 世界喊话do()
                    Do
                        If 计时器(wxNowTime) > 社交世界喊话时间间隔 Then
                            Exit Do
                        End If

                        If 好友消息通知() = 1 Then
                            Exit Do
